# checker_types triage 2: all failing cases at 05a05dc3

Tree: `/Users/mohebifar/dev/codemod/tsr-integ` at `05a05dc3` (2026-10-06 03:41, "property-4: readonly tuple element writes"). The release `verdictdump`/`casedelta`/`probefile` were copied to /tmp and run from the worktree; `casedelta` reproduces the committed snapshot exactly (**7928/9538 = 83.12%**, 469,138/478,855 lines). Upstream oracle: a copy of the pinned `vendor/typescript-go` (`5b1047d`) running `TestLocal` on ad-hoc files (`/tmp/tt2-oracle/oracle.sh`).

Population: **1610 failing cases**, **8832 aligned non-RIGHT lines** (GAP 1037, WRONG 7795). 86 cases also carry unaligned lines (54 of them have only unaligned lines); they cannot be finished by any cluster alone and are listed at the end. 13 more cases are excluded from every "finished alone" count because a verifier showed one of their lines needs a second port (listed near the end).

Result: every one of the 8832 lines is keyed (0 UNATTRIBUTED after wave 2). 518 clusters; 1143 of the 1610 failing cases are finishable by a single cluster. All 32 top-ranked clusters are verified (**V**).

## Method

1. `TSR_VERDICT_EXPR=1 verdictdump` gave every aligned line's verdict and expression; `casedelta` gave the failing-case set and, by difference, each case's unaligned-line count (`total - matched - non-RIGHT aligned`).
2. **Wave 1:** 30 classifiers each took ~54 cases (packets sorted by source path, so related cases share a packet) and keyed every non-RIGHT line to the upstream operation whose faithful port makes it RIGHT, reusing the prior triage's 297 keys (`docs/parity/types-triage.md`) where the operation was the same, sharing newly minted keys through an append-only registry, and checking most new keys with `probefile` repros against the tsgo oracle. 490 raw keys; 19 duplicate keys were folded into their canonical key, and `LITERAL-WIDENING` was split between `GET-WIDENED-LITERAL-TYPE-UNION-ARM` and `CHECK-EXPRESSION-FOR-MUTABLE-LOCATION-CONTEXT` (e.g. `ARG-FINAL-CONTEXT-RECHECK`+`GENERIC-ARG-FINAL-RECHECK` -> `ARG-CONTEXT-RESOLVED-SIG`; `TUPLE-OPTIONAL-ELEMENT-UNDEFINED` -> `-OPTIONALITY`; `DECLNAME-LIB-MERGED-SYMBOL` -> `DECL-NAME-MERGED-SYMBOL`; the three SpreadAssignment contextual keys; `CONTEXTUAL-MODULE-EXPORTS-ASSIGN` -> `CONTEXTUAL-TYPE-FOR-ASSIGNMENT-EXPRESSION-JS`; `GET-CONTEXTUAL-TYPE-ARROW-BODY-RETURN-ARM` -> `FUNCEXPR-NIL-CONTEXTUAL-SIGNATURE-POSITIONS`).
3. **Wave 2:** 26 verifiers took the 41 largest clusters (every cluster that ranked in the top 32 at any point, plus UNATTRIBUTED). Each one checked 2–3 examples end to end (source, baseline, TSR probe, tsgo code, grep-verified TSR function), scanned every line for misfits (346 lines remapped), split symptom lists into per-operation sub-keys `KEY/ARM` (802 lines moved), and spot-checked at least 3 finished-alone cases. Clusters marked **V** passed this step; **1** means wave-1 only (usually one oracle-confirmed repro).
4. Scoring is per line, as in the prior triage. A case is **blocked** by every cluster that owns at least one of its lines. It is **finished alone** by cluster X only when all its non-RIGHT lines map to X, it has no unaligned lines, and no verifier flagged a second port on one of its lines.
5. Owner flags: `ALIAS-ADR-0045` (type-alias attribute, ADR-0045 / tsr-2zk.13 TYPE-ALIAS), `MODULE-AUGMENTATION` (tsr-2zk.38), `SPURIOUS-ANY` (tsr-2zk.31), `SYMBOL-CHAIN` (getAccessibleSymbolChain/trySymbolTable printer, tsr-2zk.39), `JSDOC-REPARSER` (tsr-2zk.34). The verifier sets the flag on V clusters. On wave-1 clusters it is set when at least half of the classifiers that used the key set it.

Raw artifacts (not tracked): `/tmp/verdicts2.tsv`, `/tmp/casedelta2.tsv`, `/tmp/tt2/packets/*.md`, `/tmp/tt2/out/p*.{lines,clusters}.jsonl` (wave 1), `/tmp/tt2/verify/out/*.json` (wave 2), `/tmp/tt2/merged_final.json` (final per-line map `LV`).

### 1. `SIGNATURE-DECLARATION-ANNOTATION-REUSE` (V; blocked 92, finished alone 45, lines 296; confidence high)

- **Root cause:** One upstream op: serializeTypeForDeclaration (params) / serializeReturnTypeForSignature (returns) ask the pseudochecker for the declaration's written type node and, when pseudoTypeEquivalentToType holds, re-emit it via reuseTypeNode/nodecopy (written union order, parens, Array<X>, alias/typeof names, `true \| false`, unreduced `"A"\|"A"`, lib annotations too). TSR's written_annotation_text is a shape whitelist (alias-of-alias, string-mapping, conditional, single-quote/array-head/void-union flags, same-set top-level union only when written_type_text can render it) and otherwise prints the resolved type. Lib vs user, return vs param are the same gate, not separate ops.
- **tsgo:** internal/checker/nodebuilderimpl.go:2181 serializeTypeForDeclaration (reuse arm :2235-2256); :2023 serializeReturnTypeForSignature (pc.GetReturnTypeOfSignature + equivalence); pseudotypenodebuilder.go:15 pseudoTypeToNodeWithCheckerFallback, :362 pseudoTypeEquivalentToType; nodecopy.go:12 reuseNode
- **TSR:** crates/tsr-checker/src/signatures.rs:5010 written_annotation_text (callers parameter_of :4897/:4929, written_return :1591); union gate :5226 same_union_set_different_order; declared.rs:2701, objects.rs:1443, printing.rs:585
- **Examples:**
  - `compiler/inferenceContextualReturnTypeUnion1:0:11`: source `declare function ex3(callback: ((x: number) => void) \| 5): void`; want `(callback: ((x: number) => void) \| 5) => void`, got `(callback: 5 \| ((x: number) => void)) => void`. tsgo: pseudochecker type of param = written union node, equivalent -> reuseTypeNode keeps written order (oracle confirmed; `>callback` symbol line prints sorted). TSR: written_annotation_text reaches the §137 union gate but written_type_text can't render the function-type member -> None -> type_to_string sorted (probe confirmed).
  - `compiler/json.stringify:0:2`: source `lib: stringify(value: any, replacer?: (number \| string)[] \| null, ...)`; want `replacer?: (number \| string)[] \| null`, got `replacer?: (string \| number)[] \| null`. tsgo reuses lib annotation the same way (oracle: Object.entries `{ [s: string]: T; } \| ArrayLike<T>` too). TSR: top-level union gate compares sets but the array-of-paren-union arm only fires for a bare ArrayTypeNode, so `X[] \| null` falls through to the sorted render. Same op, whitelist gap.
- **Port notes:** Replace the whitelist with pseudochecker GetTypeOfDeclaration/GetReturnTypeOfSignature + pseudoTypeEquivalentToType, then reuse via nodecopy (incl. trackExistingEntityName). ### 2. `TYPE-ALIAS-DECLARED-BODY-AND-INSTANTIATION` (V; blocked 77, finished alone 25, lines 440; owner ALIAS-ADR-0045; confidence high)

- **Root cause:** tsgo: declared type of a generic alias = getTypeFromTypeNode(body) (alias attribute only when the body mints a new type); each reference = getTypeAliasInstantiation -> instantiateTypeWithAlias(body, mapper). TSR mints an opaque Named `Name<Params>` declared type and opaque (symbol,args) references, re-evaluated syntactically only in special cases. Printing failures (Name<Args> vs body) and semantic failures (contextual sigs, narrowing, inference, element access, spread through the opaque shell) are one root cause.
- **tsgo:** internal/checker/checker.go:23837 getDeclaredTypeOfTypeAlias; :23641 getTypeAliasInstantiation; :22104 instantiateTypeWithAlias; :23580 getTypeFromTypeAliasReference
- **TSR:** crates/tsr-checker/src/declared.rs:6667 get_declared_type_of_type_alias; :4397 get_instantiated_type_reference; :7213 evaluate_conditional_alias; :8159 evaluate_alias_body; crates/tsr-checker/src/contextual.rs:937-945 contextual_call_signature declines on Named TYPE_ALIAS shell
- **Examples:**
  - `conformance/tsxStatelessFunctionComponentOverload5:0:55`: source `onClick: MouseEventHandler<any> = EventHandler<MouseEvent<T>> (alias of {m(e):void}['m'])`; want `(e: React.MouseEvent<any>) => void`, got `any`. Repro a.ts `let a: MEH<any> = (e)=>{}`: oracle (e: { t: any; }) => void; probe error/e:any. tsgo instantiates the body to a function type so getContextualSignature finds it; TSR's contextual type is the opaque Named alias shell and contextual.rs:937-945 returns None. Same for `let f4: Ref<B> = (input)=>{}` (union body): oracle input: B, TSR any. Semantic arm, same port.
  - `compiler/varianceReferences:0:2`: source `type VarianceConstrainedNumber<V extends number> = NumericConstraint<V> (chain to V)`; want `1`, got `VarianceConstrainedNumber<1>`. tsgo: body resolves to the type param, instantiateTypeWithAlias returns the arg 1 (no alias since T is not a new type). TSR get_instantiated_type_reference returns an opaque Named reference. Probe: direct `type VU<T>=T; let vu1: VU<1>` is already right in TSR (special case), the alias chain is not.
- **Port notes:** Not two ports: semantic arms (contextual sigs for Ref/MouseEventHandler, typeof narrowing of ClassNameArg<S>, Omit spread, element access via NestedKeyOf constraint, inference vs Maybe<string>) all vanish once references are instantiated bodies; then delete the contextual.rs:937 decline and the syntactic special cases (evaluate_conditional_alias bare-node limit, evaluate_alias_body). Pitfall: alias attaches only when the body creates a new type (indexed-access/T/& {} reduce away); NonNullable must keep its alias.

### 3. `DECL-NAME-MERGED-SYMBOL` (V; blocked 34, finished alone 25, lines 58; confidence high)

- **Root cause:** tsgo getTypeOfNode declaration-name arm types getSymbolOfDeclaration(decl) = getMergedSymbol(getLateBoundSymbol(node.Symbol())), so a local decl merged with lib/other script files/augmentations prints the merged overload set/type. TSR types_producer declaration-name arm calls checker.get_type_of_symbol(binder.symbol_of(parent)) with no merged_symbol, so only the local declaration's signatures/type show (references already merged).
- **tsgo:** internal/checker/checker.go:31927 getTypeOfNode -> :14390 getSymbolOfDeclaration -> :14355 getMergedSymbol
- **TSR:** crates/tsr-conformance/src/types_producer.rs:781-783 type_id_at_location_tracking decl-name arm (binder.symbol_of, no binder.merged_symbol; compare :757 which does merge)
- **Examples:**
  - `compiler/collisionThisExpressionAndLocalVarInLambda:0:0`: source `declare function alert(message?: any): void (script, merges lib alert)`; want `{ (message?: any): void; (message?: any): void; }`, got `(message?: any) => void`. Probe repro: decl name prints (message?: any) => void while reference `alert` prints the 2-overload merged type. tsgo getSymbolOfDeclaration merges with lib alert; TSR :781 types unmerged binder symbol.
  - `compiler/duplicateVarsAcrossFileBoundaries:1:0`: source `file0 var x=3; file1 var x=true`; want `number`, got `boolean`. Probe repro (2 script files): second decl `x` prints boolean. tsgo merged global symbol's type = first declaration's (number); TSR types file1's own binder symbol from its initializer.
- **Port notes:** At types_producer.rs:781 wrap with binder.merged_symbol (tsgo also getLateBoundSymbol). Merged overload order is lib-first (merge order), type params renamed U_1 on clash — printer must handle. ### 5. `ARG-CONTEXT-RESOLVED-SIG` (V; blocked 28, finished alone 18, lines 85; confidence high)

TSR records the first-pass (uninstantiated/inferential or first-overload) check, so literals widen. The merge of ARG-FINAL-CONTEXT-RECHECK + GENERIC-ARG-FINAL-RECHECK is one op, except the `this` lines (split).

Repro g<T,U>(x:[T,U]); g(["",true]): tsgo resolves sig [string,boolean], rechecks arg with that context -> true kept => [string, true]. TSR prints [string, boolean]; non-generic h(x:[string,boolean]) gives [string, true] in TSR too, so only the resolved-signature context road is missing.

Repro: tsgo getResolvedSignature = 2nd overload, context {a:boolean} -> a: true. TSR keeps the type from checking under the first candidate/no context -> boolean.
- **Port notes:** At writer time, an argument node's type must be checked against getContextualTypeForArgumentAtIndex of the RESOLVED signature (overload chosen + instantiated), not the first-pass/uninstantiated context; nested object/array literals inherit it. typeParameterConstModifiers:259 (const T, rest [unknown,...T,unknown]) fits but also depends on const-context handling of the instantiated param.

- **Root cause:** getContextualTypeForInitializerExpression: when getContextualTypeForVariableLikeDeclaration yields nil and the declaration name is a non-empty binding pattern, the initializer's contextual type is getTypeFromBindingPattern(name, includePatternInType=true): tuple for array patterns (incl. rest), optional props for defaults, default types (literal-preserving via isLiteralOfContextualType), through parens. OBJLIT-PATTERN-OPTIONAL and BINDING-PATTERN-CONTEXT-LITERAL are the same op.
- **tsgo:** internal/checker/checker.go:29423 getContextualTypeForInitializerExpression (+ :17904 getTypeFromBindingPattern, :17957 getTypeFromArrayBindingPattern, :18006 getTypeFromBindingElement, :25522 isLiteralOfContextualType)
- **TSR:** crates/tsr-checker/src/contextual.rs:1143 get_contextual_type VariableDeclaration arm (annotation only), :1150 ParameterDeclaration arm (annotation only); approximations objects.rs:640 contextual_binding_pattern (parameter IIFE gate :672), array_literals.rs:84 destructuring_array_pattern_slot (rest gate :97/:141)
- **Examples:**

TSR: destructuring_array_pattern_slot rejects patterns with dot_dot_dot (array_literals.rs:97) and contextual.rs:1143 has no pattern fallback -> number[].
  - `compiler/classExpressionNames:0:23`: source `const { B = class {} } = ({ B: undefined })`; want `{ B?: undefined; }`, got `{ B: undefined; }`. tsgo: pattern-implied type is the contextual type of the initializer and flows through the ParenthesizedExpression arm; checkObjectLiteral marks B optional from the pattern (oracle-confirmed). TSR: contextual_binding_pattern (objects.rs:645) only matches a literal whose direct parent is the VariableDeclaration, so parens lose it.
- **Port notes:** Add the pattern fallback inside get_contextual_type for VariableDeclaration/Parameter/BindingElement initializers (skip under SkipBindingPatterns), returning binding_pattern_implied_type with pattern attached; then delete objects.rs contextual_binding_pattern and array_literals.rs slot. Pitfalls: binding_pattern_implied_type returns None on context-dependent defaults (binding_patterns.rs:124, e.g. `fn2 = fn1`) and on non-last rest (:100) — tsgo treats a non-last rest as a plain element; needs ES5 any[] vs Iterable for rest-only.

### 11. `GET-SYMBOL-CHAIN-NEEDS-QUALIFICATION` (V; blocked 16, finished alone 13, lines 29; owner SYMBOL-CHAIN; confidence medium)

- **Root cause:** Symptom bucket for getSymbolChain/getAccessibleSymbolChain. Main key (left lines): needsQualification walks raw in-scope tables by NAME (no enum members, alias resolved unless export-specifier) and qualifies when a different same-meaning symbol holds the name; TSR needs_qualification is resolve_name(...) != symbol (None => qualify), conflating shadowing with reachability; honest is_shadowed_at exists but is unwired.
- **tsgo:** internal/checker/nodebuilderimpl.go:1087 getSymbolChain; internal/checker/symbolaccessibility.go:688 needsQualification, :535 trySymbolTable
- **TSR:** crates/tsr-checker/src/checker.rs:3172 needs_qualification; :3325 is_shadowed_at (unwired); :2960 symbol_chain
- **Examples:**
  - `conformance/exportsAndImports3:1:5`: source `t2.ts `export { C1 as C } from "./t1"``; want `typeof import("./t1").C`, got `typeof C`. tsgo trySymbolTable :573 skips aliases with ExportSpecifier declarations, so t2 has no in-scope name -> chain climbs to module -> import("./t1").C. TSR symbol_chain :3057 imported_here gate suppresses the import() spelling whenever the file mentions ./t1. Repro (scratch a.ts) reproduces: oracle t2 `typeof import("./t1").C`, TSR `typeof C`.
  - `conformance/typeAliasesDoNotMerge:0:0`: source `export type A + duplicate local type A`; want `import("./typeAliasesDoNotMerge").A`, got `A`. needsQualification :692 finds a different symbol named A in file locals with Type meaning -> qualify via module. TSR needs_qualification compares resolve_name result which returns the exported one -> no qualification.
- **Port notes:** Port as 3-4 separate changes: (1) replace needs_qualification with table-walk needsQualification (is_shadowed_at); prior attempt measured 38 W->R/25 R->W, so wire with getQualifiedLeftMeaning. (2) drop imported_here gate, add ExportSpecifier exclusion in alias loop. (3) per-reference qualification inside nested type nodes. Pitfall: (2) depends on full resolveAlias for import-of-reexport, else constEnumNoEmitReexport 6/7 regress.

### 12. `TYPE-LITERAL-PROPERTY-ANNOTATION-REUSE` (V; blocked 22, finished alone 12, lines 57; confidence high)

- **Root cause:** addPropertyToElementList prints each type-literal property at PRINT time via serializeTypeForDeclaration(prop) which, when the pseudochecker's Direct type of the PropertySignature annotation is equivalent (identity/regular-literal/union-identical/error-type), reuses the written node through the existing-node visitor (entity names re-tracked at the print site, untyped params -> any). TSR bakes `printed` = type_to_string(resolved type) at build_type_literal, admitting written text only for TypeLiteral/Array/same-set-reordered Union shapes.

- **Examples:**
  - `conformance/callChain.2:0:9`: source `// @strict:false; declare const o3: { b: (() => { c: string }) \| undefined }`; want `{ b: (() => { c: string; }) \| undefined; }`, got `{ b: () => { c: string; }; }`. tsgo: addPropertyToElementList -> serializeTypeForDeclaration(b); getTypeFromTypeNode(annotation) is the same type as b (undefined absorbed w/o strictNullChecks) -> pseudoTypeEquivalentToType true -> reuseTypeNode prints the written union w/ parens. TSR: build_type_literal union gate (written_annotation_text §137) only admits same-set reorders -> type_to_string drops `\| undefined`. Repro mix.ts reproduces both (oracle vs probe).
  - `compiler/typeofInObjectLiteralType:0:0`: source `var a: { b: number; c: typeof b }`; want `{ b: number; c: typeof b; }`, got `{ b: number; c: any; }`. tsgo: `typeof b` unresolved -> errorType; pseudoTypeEquivalentToType returns true for error types (:364) -> written `typeof b` reused. TSR: get_type_from_type_node answers any (not error) so the §930 unresolved-spelling arm (declared.rs:2651) never fires; printed = type_to_string(any). Same port fixes typeof val/typeof undefined/typeof child lines.
- **Port notes:** Port serializeTypeForDeclaration for property symbols at PRINT time: pseudo Direct annotation, equivalence (identity, error-type charity, regular-literal, union identity, optional NEUndefined strip), then reuse via an existing-node visitor that re-tracks entity names at the print site (React.ReactNode, Enum-in-other-file -> fallback) and fills untyped params with any. Baking written text in build_type_literal is insufficient for cross-file/intersection sites.

TSR: type_to_string_at_worker :2252 module_name_at -> module_alias_at walks globals, Foo resolves to the module, accepted -> typeof Foo (probe confirmed).

TSR picks UMD React. Repro /tmp/tt2/scratch/v-V02/u15.ts: with `export as namespace React` TSR prints typeof React, deleting that line TSR prints typeof Element (= tsgo) -> the UMD alias is the sole cause.
- **Port notes:** Add best_name's NamespaceExportDeclaration-in-external-module filter (:4120) to module_alias_at's candidate loop. Lines needing more: reactImportDropped 1:0,1:1,1:3,2:0,2:4 need symbol_chain to spell ../../react (2:0 ../../../react.js) via module_specifier_for_symbol; importShouldNotBeElided 0:0, stackDepthLimitCastingType 1:4, checkerInitializationCrash 3:0 need SPECIFIER/NODE-MODULES.

### 17. `CONTEXTUAL-SIGNATURE-TYPEPARAM-ADOPTION` (V; blocked 16, finished alone 10, lines 46; confidence high)

- **Root cause:** assignContextualParameterTypes sets sig.typeParameters = context.typeParameters when the contextual signature is generic; the function's own signature becomes generic (<T>(x: T) => T) and outer inference sees a generic arg (S -> unknown). TSR builds type params only from own syntax/JSDoc, so it prints (x: S) => S; and when params are unannotated the grounded gate in get_type_of_function_expression sees params mentioning type params and returns error.
- **tsgo:** internal/checker/checker.go:10349 assignContextualParameterTypes (adoption :10350-10356) from :10152 contextuallyCheckFunctionExpressionOrObjectLiteralMethod
- **TSR:** crates/tsr-checker/src/signatures.rs:1471-1479 get_signature_from_declaration (type_parameter_nodes = parts.type_parameters only) + :6277 grounded check in get_type_of_function_expression (:6225)
- **Examples:**
  - `compiler/contextualTypingWithGenericSignature:0:5`: source `var f2: <T, U>(x: T, y: U) => T = (x, y) => { return x } (probe f.ts)`; want `<T, U>(x: T, y: U) => T`, got `error`. tsgo: contextual sig generic -> adopt <T,U>, params T,U. TSR: params typed T/U from context but contextual params mention type params -> grounded=false -> :6299 error.
  - `conformance/genericFunctionParameters:0:12`: source `declare function g1<T>(cb: <S>(x: S) => T): T; g1(x => x)`; want `<S>(x: S) => S (x1: unknown)`, got `(x: S) => S (x1: S)`. tsgo adopts <S>; inference of T from a generic arg signature yields unknown. TSR signature has no type params (signatures.rs:1471) so T infers the free S.
- **Port notes:** Port adoption into the signature of a context-sensitive function (cache per signature like upstream's 'already has typeParameters' early return). The grounded gate (:6277) must treat the context's OWN adopted type params as grounded, distinct from outer type params (FUNCEXPR-GROUNDED-GATE-OUTER-TYPEPARAMS). Inference: a generic arg signature must be handled as upstream (getErasedSignature/unknown).
- **Finished alone (10):** compiler/contextualTypingWithGenericSignature, compiler/contextuallyTypedGenericAssignment, compiler/genericCallWithinOwnBodyCastTypeParameterIdentity, compiler/genericFunctionHasFreshTypeArgs, compiler/genericTypeAssertions3, compiler/implicitAnyGenericTypeInference, compiler/importTypeGenericArrowTypeParenthesized, compiler/maxConstraints, conformance/genericContextualTypes1, conformance/genericFunctionParameters
- **Also blocked (6):** compiler/contextualOuterTypeParameters (+MAPPED-CONDITIONAL-WRITTEN-TEXT-MINT/CONDITIONAL-NODE); compiler/genericFunctionsNotContextSensitive (+INFER-TYPE-ARGUMENTS-CONTEXTUAL-ARGUMENT-PASSES); compiler/promisePermutations (+CHOOSE-OVERLOAD-GENERIC-WALK, DECL-NAME-MERGED-SYMBOL); compiler/promisePermutations2 (+DECL-NAME-MERGED-SYMBOL); compiler/promisePermutations3 (+CHOOSE-OVERLOAD-GENERIC-WALK, DECL-NAME-MERGED-SYMBOL); conformance/dependentDestructuredVariables (+DESTRUCTURE-FLOW-DEPENDENT, NARROWABLE-REF-BINDING-PATTERN-CONTEXT, NARROWABLE-REF-CONTEXTUAL, SIGNATURE-DECLARATION-ANNOTATION-REUSE, TYPE-REFERENCE-TO-TYPE-NODE-DEFAULT-ARG-ELISION)

### 18. `EMPTY-NAMED-FUNCTION-SYMBOL-TYPE` (V; blocked 13, finished alone 10, lines 15; confidence high)

- **Root cause:** tsgo getTypeOfFuncClassEnumModuleWorker builds an anonymous object type for any function/method symbol regardless of name; TSR's worker returns error early when has_a_name_no_type_query_can_spell (name empty or __class) and the symbol is not a nameable class expression, so parser-recovered nameless functions/methods and `[""]` computed-name methods (symbol name "") print error/any.
- **tsgo:** internal/checker/checker.go:16912 getTypeOfFuncClassEnumModuleWorker
- **TSR:** crates/tsr-checker/src/symbols.rs:3565-3573 get_type_of_func_class_enum_module_worker gate; :3844 has_a_name_no_type_query_can_spell
- **Examples:**
  - `conformance/computedPropertyNames10_ES6:0:20`: source `var v = { [""]() { } }`; want `() => void`, got `error`. Probe: `[""] : error` while other computed methods print () => void. Symbol name is "" -> TSR gate :3565 returns error; tsgo worker has no name gate -> anonymous fn type.
  - `compiler/reservedWords3:0:4`: source `function f3(function) {} (recovered nameless function)`; want `() => any`, got `any`. Probe prints `> : error` for nameless recovered function decls; tsgo types them via worker as () => any.
- **Port notes:** Restrict the gate to the TYPEOF-print legs (class/enum/value-module need a spellable name); function/method symbols must fall through to the anonymous-object branch even with empty name. Keep __class handling.
- **Finished alone (10):** conformance/FunctionPropertyAssignments2_es6, conformance/FunctionPropertyAssignments3_es6, conformance/FunctionPropertyAssignments6_es6, conformance/MemberFunctionDeclaration4_es6, conformance/MemberFunctionDeclaration5_es6, conformance/computedPropertyNames10_ES6, conformance/computedPropertyNames13_ES6, conformance/computedPropertyNames40_ES6, conformance/computedPropertyNames41_ES6, conformance/parserEqualsGreaterThanAfterFunction1
- **Also blocked (3):** compiler/reservedWords2 (+PARSE-NAME-OF-PARAMETER-MODIFIER-SKIP, SYMBOL-NAME-AS-WRITTEN-SOURCE-TEXT, unaligned); compiler/reservedWords3 (+SYMBOL-NAME-AS-WRITTEN-SOURCE-TEXT); conformance/FunctionPropertyAssignments4_es6 (+PARSER-ASTERISK-METHOD)

### 19. `MAPPED-CONDITIONAL-WRITTEN-TEXT-MINT` (V; blocked 21, finished alone 9, lines 117; confidence high)

- **Root cause:** Symptom cluster over one TSR shortcut: declared.rs:651 arm mints mapped/conditional nodes as print-only named types from written_type_text. Main (lines left): a NON-generic mapped node is an ObjectFlagsMapped object; createTypeNodeFromObjectType prints resolveMappedTypeMembers' members (index infos for string/any keys) and the relater/narrowing/contextual typing read them. TSR prints written `{ [K in ...]: ... }` and relation/narrowing/contextual property reads miss.
- **tsgo:** internal/checker/checker.go:24255 getTypeFromMappedTypeNode; :20894 resolveMappedTypeMembers; nodebuilderimpl.go:2690 createTypeNodeFromObjectType (!isGenericMappedType -> resolveStructuredTypeMembers)
- **TSR:** crates/tsr-checker/src/declared.rs:651 get_type_from_type_node Mapped\|Conditional arm (:698 written_type_text, :718 store.new_named(OBJECT,text), :774 create_semantic_mapped_type fallback); mapped.rs:45 create_semantic_mapped_type (also new_named with text); mapped.rs:447 resolve_mapped_type_members (members exist for property access but printer prints the name)
- **Examples:**
  - `compiler/correlatedUnions:0:281`: source `const funs: { [P in Keys]: (...args: ArgMap[P]) => void }`; want `{ concat: (a: string, b: string, c: string) => void; sum: (a: number, b: number) => void; }`, got `{ [P in "concat" \| "sum"]: (...args: ArgMap[P]) => void; }`. tsgo: getTypeFromMappedTypeNode -> MappedType, constraint non-generic so createTypeNodeFromObjectType prints resolveMappedTypeMembers. TSR: arm mints new_named(text) (declared.rs:718); printer emits name. Repro a.ts: `declare const m1:{[K in "a"\|"b"]:number}` TSR `{ [K in "a" \| "b"]: number; }` vs tsgo `{ a: number; b: number; }`; m1.a is number in both (members resolve, print does not).
  - `conformance/mappedTypeWithAny:0:5`: source `let x2: { [P in string]: Item }`; want `{ [x: string]: Item; }`, got `{ [P in string]: Item; }`. resolveMappedTypeMembers turns a string key into an index info; repro m4 `{[P in string]: number}` tsgo `{ [x: string]: number; }`, TSR written text.
- **Port notes:** Replace the text mint with real Mapped/Conditional type objects (MappedType w/ declaration+mapper, ConditionalRoot w/ outer params) and print via createTypeNodeFromObjectType/createMappedTypeNodeFromType/conditionalTypeToTypeNode. Keep §909 non-generic-alias naming and §905.1 recursive-alias any (DECLARED-TYPE-OF-TYPE-ALIAS-CIRCULARITY). Conditionals must carry CONDITIONAL flag (today OBJECT, declared.rs:716) so union sort/parens (PRINT-TYPE-PRECEDENCE, likely mergeable) work.
- **Split off (own rows):** `MAPPED-CONDITIONAL-WRITTEN-TEXT-MINT/CONDITIONAL-NODE` (62), `MAPPED-CONDITIONAL-WRITTEN-TEXT-MINT/MAPPED-NODE-UNDER-ALIAS-BINDINGS` (54), `MAPPED-CONDITIONAL-WRITTEN-TEXT-MINT/MAPPED-GENERIC-PRINT-FROM-PARTS` (36), `MAPPED-CONDITIONAL-WRITTEN-TEXT-MINT/MAPPED-GENERIC-INDEXED-ACCESS-CONSTRAINT` (3)

### 20. `ARRAY-LITERAL-ERROR-ELEMENT` (V; blocked 9, finished alone 9, lines 19; confidence high)

- **Root cause:** tsgo checkArrayLiteral unions element types (an errorType element included) and always builds Array<union> -> prints any[]. TSR's plain array path returns intrinsics.error the moment any element is error ('a gap in an element is a gap in the array'), so the literal, its variable, and enclosing object literal print error/any. Elements here are genuine errorType in tsgo too (jsx <meta> baseline prints `error`; tagged template on non-callable; circular alias; missing imports).
- **tsgo:** internal/checker/checker.go:8021 checkArrayLiteral (8096 getUnionTypeEx, 8100 createArrayLiteralType(createArrayTypeEx))
- **TSR:** crates/tsr-checker/src/array_literals.rs:1144 check_array_literal_value (plain path `if element_type == error { return error }`; siblings 818/831/857 spread path, 953 tuple ctx, 1065/1091 tuple-spread)
- **Examples:**
  - `compiler/missingCommaInTemplateStringsArray:0:1`: source `const array = [ `a` `b` ];`; want `any[]`, got `any (probe: error)`. Repro: oracle prints `[ `a` `b` ] : any[]` with element `any`; TSR element `error` -> line 1144 returns error, so array/var print error. Unresolved identifier element ([undefinedName]) already gives any[] in TSR because it is not intrinsics.error.
  - `conformance/arbitraryModuleNamespaceIdentifiers_importEmpty:0:4`: source `import {"missing" as x,...} ; const xyz=[x,y,z]`; want `any[]`, got `any (probe error)`. Probe: x,y,z element checks yield error; literal returns error at 1144; tsgo unions errorType -> Array<errorType> = any[].
- **Port notes:** Risk: TSR uses intrinsics.error both as tsgo errorType and as 'no answer'. Removing the early return turns declined elements into confident any[]; the port must only compose when the element is a real errorType (or accept that error-as-decline elements become any[]). Apply in all arms (plain, tuple-context, spread) for consistency.
- **Finished alone (9):** compiler/jsxEmitWithAttributes, compiler/jsxFactoryAndReactNamespace, compiler/jsxFactoryIdentifier, compiler/jsxFactoryNotIdentifierOrQualifiedName, compiler/jsxFactoryNotIdentifierOrQualifiedName2, compiler/jsxFactoryQualifiedName, compiler/missingCommaInTemplateStringsArray, compiler/selfReferentialDefaultNoStackOverflow, conformance/arbitraryModuleNamespaceIdentifiers_importEmpty
- **Also blocked (0):**

What remains is split into `/REEXPORT-DECLINE-GATE` (delete a stale checker gate, `declared.rs` `alias_rooted_reference_declines`), `/AUGMENTED-SYMBOL-IMPORT-TYPE-ROOT` (printer), `DECL-NAME-MERGED-SYMBOL/MODULE-AUGMENTATION-NAME` (the writer's declaration-name arm skips `merged_symbol`), and 3 lines for the `export =`-through-alias arm.

## Caveats

- Each line is keyed to **one** operation, the one it needs first. A line that needs two ports counts toward one cluster only; where a verifier found this, the case is excluded from finished-alone (list above). Unverified (**1**) clusters may still hide such lines.
- Family splits lower the per-row finished-alone counts. `FUNCEXPR-NIL-CONTEXTUAL-SIGNATURE-POSITIONS` is the extreme case: its 7 arms are one upstream rule (`getContextualSignature` nil -> `assignNonContextualParameterTypes`), so use the port-set row when the port replaces TSR's `has_no_contextual_type` whitelist outright.
- For a few lines (e.g. `globalThisBlockscopedProperties`, `templateLiteralTypes2/3`, `renamingDestructuredPropertyInFunctionType`), `probefile` prints `error` where the corpus dump shows `any`. The corpus dump is authoritative for every count here.
- The oracle cannot load `/.lib/react.d.ts`, so React findings were confirmed on self-contained JSX-namespace repros or stripped copies of react16.d.ts.
- Counts are for `05a05dc3` and binaries built at 03:41. Lanes landing after that will move them.
